EXECUTIVE READING

先给结论，再进入源码

核心机制

一次用户输入可能触发多次“模型思考—调工具—看结果—再思考”，每一步前都留存档点。

证据规模

30 条 finding / 29 处引用

主要边界

当前源码证据仍需部署级验证

适用建设

一次用户输入可能触发多次“模型思考—调工具—看结果—再思考”，每一步前都留存档点。

值得借鉴

  • 一次用户输入可能触发多次“模型思考—调工具—看结果—再思考”，每一步前都留存档点。
  • 模型每次再开口前都会先收新通知和当前模式规则；工具可以边生成边启动，结果到齐后才记账。
  • 用户插话是在当前路线后追加新指令；D-Mail 则像读档，把对话回到旧存档再塞入一张“未来经验”纸条。

需要警惕

  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证
  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证
  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证

阅读方式

  • 先看固定提交，再看调用链
  • 事实、推断、风险分栏
  • 每条 finding 可回到源码
00 · METHOD

研究口径：不是 README 摘要

README POLICY

README 只定位入口和产品命名；实现结论必须回到运行时代码、契约或测试。

FACT POLICY

L1 运行代码优先，L2 类型/事件契约补足，L3/L4 交叉验证，L5 推断单独标记。

SNAPSHOT

统一 approval、YOLO/AFK、Plan gate、hook；本地 KAOS 直接宿主执行，非 OS 沙箱。

延迟 MCP、OAuth、输出截断、subprocess plugins、server/wire hooks。

全部指令与扩展面verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

Jinja system prompt、AGENTS 层级与预算、跨品牌 skills、slash/flow skills。

协作与子 AgentverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

coder/explore/plan、前后台执行、持久实例/resume、模型覆盖、统一 approval/wire。

观测、日志与回放verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

context/wire/state/subagent artifacts、trace/wire events、opt-out telemetry、Apache-2.0。

   21  NAME = "EnterPlanMode"

   24

   26  class Params(BaseModel):
   27      pass
   28
   29
   30  class EnterPlanMode(CallableTool2[Params]):
   31      name: str = NAME
   32      description: str = _DESCRIPTION
   33      params: type[Params] = Params
   34
   35      def __init__(self) -> None:
   36          super().__init__()
   37          self._toggle_callback: Callable[[], Awaitable[bool]] | None = None
   38          self._plan_file_path_getter: Callable[[], Path | None] | None = None
   39          self._plan_mode_checker: Callable[[], bool] | None = None
   40          self._is_auto_approve: Callable[[], bool] | None = None
   41
   42      def bind(
   43          self,
   44          toggle_callback: Callable[[], Awaitable[bool]],
   45          plan_file_path_getter: Callable[[], Path | None],
   46          plan_mode_checker: Callable[[], bool],
   47          is_auto_approve: Callable[[], bool] | None = None,
   48          *,
   49          is_yolo: Callable[[], bool] | None = None,
   50      ) -> None:
   51          """Late-bind soul callbacks after KimiSoul is constructed."""
   52          self._toggle_callback = toggle_callback
   53          self._plan_file_path_getter = plan_file_path_getter
   54          self._plan_mode_checker = plan_mode_checker
   55          self._is_auto_approve = is_auto_approve or is_yolo
   56
   57      @override
   58      async def __call__(self, params: Params) -> ToolReturnValue:
   59          # Guard: already in plan mode
   60          if self._plan_mode_checker and self._plan_mode_checker():
   61              return ToolError(
   62                  message="Already in plan mode. Use ExitPlanMode when your plan is ready.",
   63                  brief="Already in plan mode",
   64              )
   65
   66          if not self._toggle_callback or not self._plan_file_path_getter:
   67              return ToolError(
   68                  message="EnterPlanMode is not properly initialized.",
   69                  brief="Not initialized",
   70              )
   71
   72          # Auto-approve entering plan mode when approvals are bypassed.
   73          if self._is_auto_approve and self._is_auto_approve():
   74              from kimi_cli.telemetry import track
   75
   76              track("plan_enter_resolved", outcome="auto_approved")
   77              await self._toggle_callback()
   78              plan_path = self._plan_file_path_getter()
   79              return ToolReturnValue(
   80                  is_error=False,

ExitPlanMode

ExitPlanMode→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
   23      QuestionRequest,
   24  )
   25
   26  logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   27
   28  NAME = "ExitPlanMode"
   29
   30  _RESERVED_LABELS = {"reject", "revise", "approve", "reject and exit"}
   31
   32
   33  class PlanOption(BaseModel):
   34      """A selectable approach/option within the plan."""
   35
   36      label: str = Field(
   37          description=(
   38              "Short name for this option (1-8 words). "
   39              "Append '(Recommended)' if you recommend this option."
   40          ),
   41      )
   42      description: str = Field(
   43          default="",
   44          description="Brief summary of this approach and its trade-offs.",
   45      )
   46
   47      @field_validator("label")
   48      @classmethod
   49      def label_not_reserved(cls, v: str) -> str:
   50          if v.strip().lower() in _RESERVED_LABELS:
   51              reserved = ", ".join(f"'{w.title()}'" for w in sorted(_RESERVED_LABELS))
   52              raise ValueError(
   53                  f"Option label {v!r} is reserved. Do not use {reserved} as option labels."
   54              )
   55          return v
   56
   57
   58  class Params(BaseModel):
   59      options: list[PlanOption] | None = Field(
   60          default=None,
   61          max_length=3,
   62          description=(
   63              "When the plan contains multiple alternative approaches, list them here "
   64              "so the user can choose which one to execute. 2-3 options. "
   65              "Each option represents a distinct approach from the plan. "
   66              "Do not use 'Reject', 'Revise', 'Approve', or 'Reject and Exit' as labels."
   67          ),
   68      )
   69
   70      @field_validator("options")
   71      @classmethod
   72      def options_labels_unique(cls, v: list[PlanOption] | None) -> list[PlanOption] | None:
   73          if v is None:
   74              return v
   75          labels = [opt.label for opt in v]
   76          if len(labels) != len(set(labels)):
   77              raise ValueError("Option labels must be unique. Found duplicate label(s).")
   78          return v
   79
   80
   81  class ExitPlanMode(CallableTool2[Params]):
   82      name: str = NAME
   83      description: str = load_desc(Path(__file__).parent / "description.md")
   84      params: type[Params] = Params
   85
   86      def __init__(self) -> None:
   87          super().__init__()

  241  @cli.command(name="export")
  242  def export(
  243      ctx: typer.Context,
  244      session_id: Annotated[
  245          str | None,
  246          typer.Argument(help="Session ID to export. Defaults to the previous session."),
  247      ] = None,

  249          Path | None,

  251              "--output",

  262          ),

  264  ) -> None:
  265      """Export a session as a ZIP archive."""
  266      work_dir = _resolve_work_dir(ctx)
  267
  268      if session_id is None:
  269          session = asyncio.run(_load_previous_session(work_dir))
  270          if session is None:
  271              typer.echo("Error: no previous session found for the working directory.", err=True)
  272              raise typer.Exit(code=1)
  273          if not yes and not _confirm_previous_session(session):
  274              typer.echo("Export cancelled.")
  275              return
  276          session_id = session.id
  277          session_dir = session.dir
  278      else:
  279          session_dir = _find_session_by_id(session_id, work_dir=work_dir)
  280          if session_dir is None:
  281              typer.echo(f"Error: session '{session_id}' not found.", err=True)
  282              raise typer.Exit(code=1)
  283
  284      # Collect all files in the session directory (including subagents/ and tasks/)
  285      files = sorted(f for f in session_dir.rglob("*") if f.is_file())
  286      if not files:
  287          typer.echo(f"Error: session '{session_id}' has no files.", err=True)
  288          raise typer.Exit(code=1)
  289
  290      # Determine output path
  291      if output is None:
  292          output = Path.cwd() / f"session-{session_id}.zip"
  293
  294      # Collect recent global log files for diagnostics
  295      log_files = _collect_recent_log_files(session_dir)
  296
  297      # Build manifest with system diagnostics
  298      manifest = _build_manifest(session_id, session_dir)
  299
  300      # Create ZIP

  303  @registry.command(name="import")
  304  async def import_context(soul: KimiSoul, args: str):
  305      """Import context from a file or session ID"""
  306      from kimi_cli.utils.export import perform_import
  307
  308      target = sanitize_cli_path(args)
  309      if not target:
  310          wire_send(TextPart(text="Usage: /import <file_path or session_id>"))
  311          return
  312
  313      session = soul.runtime.session
  314      raw_max_context_size = (
  315          soul.runtime.llm.max_context_size if soul.runtime.llm is not None else None
  316      )
  317      max_context_size = (
  318          raw_max_context_size
  319          if isinstance(raw_max_context_size, int) and raw_max_context_size > 0
  320          else None
  321      )
  322      result = await perform_import(
  323          target=target,
  324          current_session_id=session.id,
  325          work_dir=session.work_dir,
  326          context=soul.context,
  327          max_context_size=max_context_size,
  328      )
  329      if isinstance(result, str):
  330          wire_send(TextPart(text=result))
  331          return
  332
  333      source_desc, content_len = result
  334      wire_send(TextPart(text=f"Imported context from {source_desc} ({content_len} chars)."))
  335      if source_desc.startswith("file") and is_sensitive_file(Path(target).name):
  336          wire_send(
  337              TextPart(
  338                  text="Warning: This file may contain secrets (API keys, tokens, credentials). "
  339                  "The content is now part of your session context."
  340              )
  341          )

install

CLI 子命令 · command registration · src/kimi_cli/cli/plugin.py:228
源码 ↗

Plugin source: directory, .zip file, .zip URL, or git URL

install→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  223          err=True,
  224      )
  225      raise typer.Exit(1)
  226
  227
  228  @cli.command("install")
  229  def install_cmd(
  230      target: Annotated[
  231          str,
  232          typer.Argument(help="Plugin source: directory, .zip file, .zip URL, or git URL"),
  233      ],
  234  ) -> None:
  235      """Install a plugin and inject host configuration."""
  236      import shutil
  237
  238      from kimi_cli.config import load_config
  239      from kimi_cli.constant import VERSION
  240      from kimi_cli.plugin.manager import get_plugins_dir, install_plugin
  241
  242      source, tmp_dir = _resolve_source(target)
  243
  244      try:
  245          config = load_config()
  246
  247          from kimi_cli.auth.oauth import OAuthManager
  248          from kimi_cli.llm import augment_provider_with_env_vars
  249          from kimi_cli.plugin.manager import collect_host_values
  250
  251          # Apply env var overrides (install runs outside normal startup)
  252          if config.default_model and config.default_model in config.models:
  253              model = config.models[config.default_model]
  254              if model.provider in config.providers:
  255                  augment_provider_with_env_vars(config.providers[model.provider], model)
  256
  257          oauth = OAuthManager(config)
  258          host_values = collect_host_values(config, oauth)
  259
  260          if not host_values.get("api_key"):
  261              typer.echo(
  262                  "Warning: No LLM provider configured. "
  263                  "Plugins requiring API key injection will fail. "
  264                  "Run 'kimi login' or configure a provider first.",
  265                  err=True,
  266              )
  267
  268          spec = install_plugin(
  269              source=source,
  270              plugins_dir=get_plugins_dir(),
  271              host_values=host_values,
  272              host_name="kimi-code",
  273              host_version=VERSION,
  274          )
  275      except PluginError as exc:
  276          typer.echo(f"Error: {exc}", err=True)
  277          raise typer.Exit(1) from exc
  278      finally:
  279          # Clean up temp directory from zip/git extraction
  280          if tmp_dir is not None:
  281              shutil.rmtree(tmp_dir, ignore_errors=True)
  282
  283      typer.echo(f"Installed plugin '{spec.name}' v{spec.version}")
  284      if spec.runtime:
  285          typer.echo(f"  runtime: host={spec.runtime.host}, version={spec.runtime.host_version}")
  286
  287

  223  @cli.command("list")
  224  def mcp_list():
  225      """List all MCP servers."""
  226      config_file = get_global_mcp_config_file()
  227      config = _load_mcp_config()
  228      servers: dict[str, Any] = config.get("mcpServers", {})
  229
  230      typer.echo(f"MCP config file: {config_file}")
  231      if not servers:
  232          typer.echo("No MCP servers configured.")
  233          return
  234
  235      for name, server in servers.items():
  236          if "command" in server:
  237              cmd = server["command"]
  238              cmd_args = " ".join(server.get("args", []))
  239              line = f"{name} (stdio): {cmd} {cmd_args}".rstrip()
  240          elif "url" in server:
  241              transport = server.get("transport") or "http"
  242              if transport == "streamable-http":
  243                  transport = "http"
  244              line = f"{name} ({transport}): {server['url']}"
  245              if server.get("auth") == "oauth" and not _has_oauth_tokens(server["url"]):
  246                  line += " [authorization required - run: kimi mcp auth " + name + "]"
  247          else:
  248              line = f"{name}: {server}"
  249          typer.echo(f"  {line}")
  250
  251

  265          raise typer.Exit(code=1)

  241  @cli.command(name="export")
  242  def export(
  243      ctx: typer.Context,
  244      session_id: Annotated[
  245          str | None,
  246          typer.Argument(help="Session ID to export. Defaults to the previous session."),
  247      ] = None,
  248      output: Annotated[
  249          Path | None,
  250          typer.Option(
  251              "--output",
  252              "-o",
  253              help="Output ZIP file path. Default: session-{id}.zip in current directory.",
  254          ),
  255      ] = None,
  256      yes: Annotated[
  257          bool,
  258          typer.Option(
  259              "--yes",
  260              "-y",
  261              help="Skip confirmation when exporting the previous session by default.",
  262          ),
  263      ] = False,
  264  ) -> None:
  265      """Export a session as a ZIP archive."""
  266      work_dir = _resolve_work_dir(ctx)
  267
  268      if session_id is None:
  269          session = asyncio.run(_load_previous_session(work_dir))
  270          if session is None:
  271              typer.echo("Error: no previous session found for the working directory.", err=True)
  272              raise typer.Exit(code=1)
  273          if not yes and not _confirm_previous_session(session):
  274              typer.echo("Export cancelled.")
  275              return
  276          session_id = session.id
  277          session_dir = session.dir
  278      else:
  279          session_dir = _find_session_by_id(session_id, work_dir=work_dir)
  280          if session_dir is None:
  281              typer.echo(f"Error: session '{session_id}' not found.", err=True)
  282              raise typer.Exit(code=1)
  283
  284      # Collect all files in the session directory (including subagents/ and tasks/)
  285      files = sorted(f for f in session_dir.rglob("*") if f.is_file())
  286      if not files:
  287          typer.echo(f"Error: session '{session_id}' has no files.", err=True)
  288          raise typer.Exit(code=1)
  289
  290      # Determine output path
  291      if output is None:
  292          output = Path.cwd() / f"session-{session_id}.zip"
  293
  294      # Collect recent global log files for diagnostics
  295      log_files = _collect_recent_log_files(session_dir)
  296
  297      # Build manifest with system diagnostics
  298      manifest = _build_manifest(session_id, session_dir)
  299
  300      # Create ZIP

  319  @cli.command("info")
  320  def info_cmd(
  321      name: Annotated[str, typer.Argument(help="Plugin name")],
  322  ) -> None:
  323      """Show plugin details."""
  324      from kimi_cli.plugin import parse_plugin_json
  325      from kimi_cli.plugin.manager import get_plugins_dir
  326
  327      plugin_json = get_plugins_dir() / name / "plugin.json"
  328      if not plugin_json.exists():
  329          typer.echo(f"Error: Plugin '{name}' not found", err=True)
  330          raise typer.Exit(1)
  331
  332      try:
  333          spec = parse_plugin_json(plugin_json)
  334      except PluginError as exc:
  335          typer.echo(f"Error: {exc}", err=True)
  336          raise typer.Exit(1) from exc
  337
  338      typer.echo(f"Name:        {spec.name}")
  339      typer.echo(f"Version:     {spec.version}")
  340      typer.echo(f"Description: {spec.description or '(none)'}")
  341      typer.echo(f"Config file: {spec.config_file or '(none)'}")
  342      if spec.inject:
  343          typer.echo(f"Inject:      {', '.join(f'{k} <- {v}' for k, v in spec.inject.items())}")
  344      if spec.runtime:
  345          typer.echo(f"Runtime:     host={spec.runtime.host}, version={spec.runtime.host_version}")
  346      else:
  347          typer.echo("Runtime:     (not installed via host)")

/install

交互 slash 指令 · decorated slash handler · src/kimi_cli/cli/plugin.py:228
源码 ↗

Plugin source: directory, .zip file, .zip URL, or git URL

/install→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  223          err=True,
  224      )
  225      raise typer.Exit(1)
  226
  227
  228  @cli.command("install")
  229  def install_cmd(
  230      target: Annotated[
  231          str,
  232          typer.Argument(help="Plugin source: directory, .zip file, .zip URL, or git URL"),
  233      ],
  234  ) -> None:
  235      """Install a plugin and inject host configuration."""
  236      import shutil
  237
  238      from kimi_cli.config import load_config
  239      from kimi_cli.constant import VERSION
  240      from kimi_cli.plugin.manager import get_plugins_dir, install_plugin
  241
  242      source, tmp_dir = _resolve_source(target)
  243
  244      try:
  245          config = load_config()
  246
  247          from kimi_cli.auth.oauth import OAuthManager
  248          from kimi_cli.llm import augment_provider_with_env_vars
  249          from kimi_cli.plugin.manager import collect_host_values
  250
  251          # Apply env var overrides (install runs outside normal startup)
  252          if config.default_model and config.default_model in config.models:
  253              model = config.models[config.default_model]
  254              if model.provider in config.providers:
  255                  augment_provider_with_env_vars(config.providers[model.provider], model)
  256
  257          oauth = OAuthManager(config)
  258          host_values = collect_host_values(config, oauth)
  259
  260          if not host_values.get("api_key"):
  261              typer.echo(
  262                  "Warning: No LLM provider configured. "
  263                  "Plugins requiring API key injection will fail. "
  264                  "Run 'kimi login' or configure a provider first.",
  265                  err=True,
  266              )
  267
  268          spec = install_plugin(
  269              source=source,
  270              plugins_dir=get_plugins_dir(),
  271              host_values=host_values,
  272              host_name="kimi-code",
  273              host_version=VERSION,
  274          )
  275      except PluginError as exc:
  276          typer.echo(f"Error: {exc}", err=True)
  277          raise typer.Exit(1) from exc
  278      finally:
  279          # Clean up temp directory from zip/git extraction
  280          if tmp_dir is not None:
  281              shutil.rmtree(tmp_dir, ignore_errors=True)
  282
  283      typer.echo(f"Installed plugin '{spec.name}' v{spec.version}")
  284      if spec.runtime:
  285          typer.echo(f"  runtime: host={spec.runtime.host}, version={spec.runtime.host_version}")
  286
  287

  222
  223  @cli.command("list")
  224  def mcp_list():
  225      """List all MCP servers."""
  226      config_file = get_global_mcp_config_file()
  227      config = _load_mcp_config()
  228      servers: dict[str, Any] = config.get("mcpServers", {})
  229
  230      typer.echo(f"MCP config file: {config_file}")
  231      if not servers:
  232          typer.echo("No MCP servers configured.")
  233          return
  234
  235      for name, server in servers.items():
  236          if "command" in server:
  237              cmd = server["command"]
  238              cmd_args = " ".join(server.get("args", []))
  239              line = f"{name} (stdio): {cmd} {cmd_args}".rstrip()
  240          elif "url" in server:
  241              transport = server.get("transport") or "http"
  242              if transport == "streamable-http":
  243                  transport = "http"
  244              line = f"{name} ({transport}): {server['url']}"
  245              if server.get("auth") == "oauth" and not _has_oauth_tokens(server["url"]):
  246                  line += " [authorization required - run: kimi mcp auth " + name + "]"
  247          else:
  248              line = f"{name}: {server}"
  249          typer.echo(f"  {line}")
  250
  251
  252  @cli.command("auth")
  253  def mcp_auth(
  254      name: Annotated[
  255          str,
  256          typer.Argument(help="Name of the MCP server to authorize."),
  257      ],
  258  ):
  259      """Authorize with an OAuth-enabled MCP server."""
  260      import asyncio
  261
  262      server = _get_mcp_server(name, require_remote=True)
  263      if server.get("auth") != "oauth":
  264          typer.echo(f"MCP server '{name}' does not use OAuth. Add with --auth oauth.", err=True)
  265          raise typer.Exit(code=1)
  266
  267      async def _auth() -> None:
  268          import fastmcp
  269
  270          from kimi_cli.mcp_oauth import prepare_mcp_server_config
  271
  272          typer.echo(f"Authorizing with '{name}'...")
  273          typer.echo("A browser window will open for authorization.")
  274
  275          client = fastmcp.Client({"mcpServers": {name: prepare_mcp_server_config(server)}})
  276          try:
  277              async with client:
  278                  tools = await client.list_tools()
  279                  typer.echo(f"Successfully authorized with '{name}'.")
  280                  typer.echo(f"Available tools: {len(tools)}")
  281          except Exception as e:
  282              typer.echo(f"Authorization failed: {type(e).__name__}: {e}", err=True)

03 · COURSE ROUTE

十一章教程不是摘要，而是源码阅读路线

每章会从本项目的多个 finding 中挑出证据，先讲问题，再给比喻，然后把实现、边界、图谱和执行语义串起来。

M01

总览：先知道它解决什么问题

01
DIMENSION · ARCHITECTURE

架构与所有权

4 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · 每轮是带检查点的多步状态机，不是单次聊天请求

源码事实

run 先刷新 OAuth、建立 approval source、运行 UserPromptSubmit hook，再进入 _turn；_turn 先 checkpoint、持久化 user message，然后 _agent_loop 逐 step 做压缩、checkpoint、LLM+工具执行，直到无工具调用、拒绝或重复调用强停。

白话解释

一次用户输入可能触发多次“模型思考—调工具—看结果—再思考”，每一步前都留存档点。

对自研 Harness 的含义

长任务可恢复、可插话，也需要严格的步数上限和副作用治理。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 659–699
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 700–742
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  676              # Refresh OAuth tokens on each turn to avoid idle-time expirations.

  686              # --- UserPromptSubmit hook ---
  687              # Synthetic internal prompts (e.g. background-task notification

  689              # the wait ceiling is hit) must bypass ``UserPromptSubmit``:
  690              # they are not user input, and a user-configured prompt-blocking
  691              # hook would drop the notification and hang the wait loop.
  692              if not skip_user_prompt_hook:

  695                  hook_results = await self._hook_engine.trigger(

  700                          cwd=str(Path.cwd()),
  701                          prompt=text_input_for_hook,
  702                      ),
  703                  )
  704                  for result in hook_results:
  705                      if result.action == "block":
  706                          wire_send(TurnBegin(user_input=user_input))
  707                          turn_started = True
  708                          wire_send(TextPart(text=result.reason or "Prompt blocked by hook."))
  709                          wire_send(TurnEnd())
  710                          turn_finished = True
  711                          return
  712
  713              wire_send(TurnBegin(user_input=user_input))
  714              turn_started = True
  715              from kimi_cli.telemetry import track as _track_telemetry
  716
  717              _track_telemetry(
  718                  "turn_started",
  719                  mode="plan" if self._plan_mode else "agent",
  720                  **_provider_telemetry_kwargs(self._runtime.llm),
  721              )
  722              user_message = Message(role="user", content=user_input)
  723              text_input = user_message.extract_text(" ").strip()
  724
  725              if command_call := parse_slash_command_call(text_input):
  726                  command = self._find_slash_command(command_call.name)
  727                  if command is None:
  728                      # this should not happen actually, the shell should have filtered it out
  729                      wire_send(TextPart(text=f'Unknown slash command "/{command_call.name}".'))
  730                  else:
  731                      ret = command.func(self, command_call.args)
  732                      if isinstance(ret, Awaitable):
  733                          await ret
  734              elif self._loop_control.max_ralph_iterations != 0:
  735                  runner = FlowRunner.ralph_loop(
  736                      user_message,
  737                      self._loop_control.max_ralph_iterations,
  738                  )
  739                  await runner.run(self, "")
  740              else:
  741                  await self._turn(user_message)
  742
  743              # --- Stop hook (max 1 re-trigger to prevent infinite loop) ---
  744              if not self._stop_hook_active:
  745                  stop_results = await self._hook_engine.trigger(

  747                      input_data=events.stop(

  749                          cwd=str(Path.cwd()),
  750                          stop_hook_active=False,
  751                      ),
  752                  )
  753                  for result in stop_results:
  754                      if result.action == "block" and result.reason:
  755                          self._stop_hook_active = True
  756                          try:
  757                              await self._turn(Message(role="user", content=result.reason))
  758                          finally:
  759                              self._stop_hook_active = False
  760                          break

  763              turn_finished = True
  764
  765              # Auto-set title after first real turn (skip slash commands)
  766              if not command_call:

  768                  if session.state.custom_title is None:

  770
  771                      title = shorten(
  772                          Message(role="user", content=user_input).extract_text(" "),
  773                          width=50,
  774                      )
  775                      if title:

  777                              load_session_state,
  778                              save_session_state,
  779                          )
  780
  781                          # Read-modify-write: load fresh state to avoid
  782                          # overwriting concurrent web changes
  783                          fresh = load_session_state(session.dir)
  784                          if fresh.custom_title is None:
  785                              fresh.custom_title = title
  786                              save_session_state(fresh, session.dir)
  787                          session.state.custom_title = fresh.custom_title
  788          except MaxStepsReached:
  789              interrupt_reason = "max_steps"
  790              raise
  791          except asyncio.CancelledError:
  792              interrupt_reason = "user_cancelled"
  793              raise
  794          except Exception:
  795              interrupt_reason = "error"
  796              raise

  799
  800              if turn_started and not turn_finished:
  801                  wire_send(TurnEnd())
  802                  from kimi_cli.telemetry import track as _track_telemetry

  807                      interrupt_reason=interrupt_reason or "error",

  809                  )
  810                  if _tid := get_current_trace_id():
  811                      _interrupt_kwargs["trace_id"] = _tid
  812                  _track_telemetry("turn_interrupted", **_interrupt_kwargs)

02
L1factarchitecture

02 · 每 step 先注入通知/动态约束，再一次生成并等待并发工具

源码事实

root 每 step 最多领取 4 条 pending notification；Plan/AFK 等 injection 合并成 user-side system reminder；历史归一化后调用 kosong.step，流式回调消息和工具结果，随后 await 所有 tool results 并 shield 写回上下文。

白话解释

模型每次再开口前都会先收新通知和当前模式规则；工具可以边生成边启动，结果到齐后才记账。

对自研 Harness 的含义

实时性和并行性强；动态注入是上下文的一部分，会影响 token 与缓存。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1132–1162
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 1163–1194
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
L1factarchitecture

03 · steer 与 D-Mail 提供两种中途改道机制

源码事实

steer queue 会在 step 结束或相邻 step 间作为新的 user message 注入；SendDMail 可携 checkpoint id 触发 BackToTheFuture，主循环旋转并回退 context，再注入未来消息并清空重复调用状态。

白话解释

用户插话是在当前路线后追加新指令；D-Mail 则像读档，把对话回到旧存档再塞入一张“未来经验”纸条。

对自研 Harness 的含义

适合长程纠偏和探索回溯，但文件系统副作用不会随 context 自动回滚。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 622–636
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 637–653
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

边界 / 风险
  • 回滚的是对话 JSONL，不是 git/worktree 或外部服务事务。

04
L1factarchitecture

04 · 错误恢复分 OAuth、连接、空响应和 HTTP 状态码

源码事实

step/compaction 使用指数抖动重试；401 仅对 OAuth provider 强制刷新一次；连接/超时可调用 RetryableChatProvider.on_retryable_error 并只恢复一次；空响应和 429/500/502/503/504 进入 tenacity。

白话解释

不是遇错一律重试：令牌过期先换令牌，连接坏了让 provider 重建连接，服务繁忙才退避。

对自研 Harness 的含义

恢复语义清楚；408/409/529 在遥测里可标 retryable，但当前 Python 重试循环并不重试它们。

    入口、配置和状态初始化

    分支、副作用和回执

    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
DIMENSION · MODELCONTEXT

模型与上下文

7 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L2factmodelContext

01 · Kosong 把流式生成和工具调度拆成两层契约

源码事实

generate 合并流式 Text/Think/ToolCall part，拒绝完全空或仅 thinking 的异常响应；step 在完整 tool call 到达时立即交 Toolset.handle，并收集异步 futures，生成失败/取消时取消所有在途工具。

白话解释

底层负责把碎片拼成完整回复，上层负责一看到完整工具单就开工；若模型流中断，尚未完成的工具任务会被收拢取消。

对自研 Harness 的含义

Provider 和 Harness 解耦，同时避免中断后悬挂任务。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 52–76
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 77–103
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

packages/kosong/src/kosong/_generate.py:52–103固定提交 ↗
   52      message = Message(role="assistant", content=[])

   54
   55      logger.trace("Generating with history: {history}", history=history)
   56      stream = await chat_provider.generate(system_prompt, tools, history)
   57      if on_trace_id:
   58          # getattr for robustness against third-party StreamedMessage
   59          # implementations that predate the trace_id property.
   60          await callback(on_trace_id, getattr(stream, "trace_id", None))
   61      async for part in stream:
   62          logger.trace("Received part: {part}", part=part)
   63          if on_message_part:
   64              await callback(on_message_part, part.model_copy(deep=True))
   65
   66          if pending_part is None:
   67              pending_part = part
   68          elif not pending_part.merge_in_place(part):  # try merge into the pending part
   69              # unmergeable part must push the pending part to the buffer
   70              _message_append(message, pending_part)
   71              if isinstance(pending_part, ToolCall) and on_tool_call:
   72                  await callback(on_tool_call, pending_part)
   73              pending_part = part
   74
   75      # end of message
   76      if pending_part is not None:
   77          _message_append(message, pending_part)
   78          if isinstance(pending_part, ToolCall) and on_tool_call:
   79              await callback(on_tool_call, pending_part)
   80
   81      if not message.content and not message.tool_calls:
   82          raise APIEmptyResponseError("The API returned an empty response.")
   83
   84      # A response with only ThinkPart (no TextPart, no tool calls) indicates an
   85      # abnormal termination — typically a stream interruption or max_tokens
   86      # exhaustion during reasoning.  The model should always produce visible
   87      # output after thinking; a think-only response is never intentional.
   88      has_think = any(isinstance(p, ThinkPart) for p in message.content)
   89      has_text = any(isinstance(p, TextPart) and p.text.strip() for p in message.content)
   90      if has_think and not has_text and not message.tool_calls:
   91          raise APIEmptyResponseError(
   92              "The API returned a response containing only thinking content "
   93              "without any text or tool calls. This usually indicates the "
   94              "stream was interrupted or the output token budget was exhausted "
   95              "during reasoning."
   96          )
   97
   98      return GenerateResult(
   99          id=stream.id,
  100          message=message,
  101          usage=stream.usage,
  102          trace_id=getattr(stream, "trace_id", None),
  103      )
  104
  105
  106  @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
  107  class GenerateResult:
  108      """The result of a generation."""
  109
  110      id: str | None
  111      """The ID of the generated message."""
  112      message: Message
  113      """The generated message."""
  114      usage: TokenUsage | None
  115      """The token usage of the generated message."""
  116      trace_id: str | None = None
  117      """The ``x-trace-id`` response header of the request, if the provider exposes it."""
  118
  119
  120  def _message_append(message: Message, part: StreamedMessagePart) -> None:
  121      match part:
  122          case ContentPart():
  123              message.content.append(part)
  124          case ToolCall():
  125              if message.tool_calls is None:
  126                  message.tool_calls = []
  127              message.tool_calls.append(part)
  128          case _:
  129              # may be an orphaned `ToolCallPart`
  130              return
  131

02
L1factmodelContext

02 · 同一 LLM 层支持 Kimi、OpenAI、Anthropic、Gemini 与 Vertex

源码事实

create_llm 按 provider type 构造 Kimi、OpenAI legacy/Responses、Anthropic、GoogleGenAI/Vertex，以及测试 Echo/Chaos；模型 capability 决定 thinking/image 等能力，thinking 可按配置开启或关闭。

白话解释

主循环不绑某一家 API，同一套工具和上下文可换不同模型协议。

对自研 Harness 的含义

可移植性好，但 Kimi 专属 generation override、prompt cache key 与 preserved thinking 只在 Kimi provider 生效。

逐段阅读提示

    入口、配置和状态初始化

  2. 398–470
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  326  def create_llm(
  327      provider: LLMProvider,
  328      model: LLMModel,
  329      *,
  330      thinking: bool | None = None,
  331      session_id: str | None = None,
  332      oauth: OAuthManager | None = None,
  333  ) -> LLM | None:
  334      if provider.type not in {"_echo", "_scripted_echo"} and (
  335          not provider.base_url or not model.model
  336      ):
  337          logger.warning(
  338              "Cannot create LLM: missing base_url or model (provider_type={provider_type})",
  339              provider_type=provider.type,
  340          )
  341          return None
  342
  343      resolved_api_key = (
  344          oauth.resolve_api_key(provider.api_key, provider.oauth)
  345          if oauth and provider.oauth
  346          else provider.api_key.get_secret_value()
  347      )
  348
  349      match provider.type:
  350          case "kimi":
  351              from kosong.chat_provider.kimi import Kimi
  352
  353              chat_provider = Kimi(
  354                  model=model.model,
  355                  base_url=provider.base_url,
  356                  api_key=resolved_api_key,
  357                  default_headers=_kimi_default_headers(provider, oauth),
  358              )
  359
  360              gen_kwargs: Kimi.GenerationKwargs = {}
  361              if session_id:
  362                  gen_kwargs["prompt_cache_key"] = session_id
  363              if temperature := os.getenv("KIMI_MODEL_TEMPERATURE"):
  364                  gen_kwargs["temperature"] = float(temperature)
  365              if top_p := os.getenv("KIMI_MODEL_TOP_P"):
  366                  gen_kwargs["top_p"] = float(top_p)
  367              for env_name in (
  368                  "KIMI_MODEL_MAX_COMPLETION_TOKENS",
  369                  "KIMI_MODEL_MAX_TOKENS",
  370              ):
  371                  raw_max_completion_tokens = os.getenv(env_name)
  372                  if not raw_max_completion_tokens:
  373                      continue
  374                  try:
  375                      max_completion_tokens = int(raw_max_completion_tokens)
  376                  except ValueError:
  377                      continue
  378                  # ``None`` is an explicit opt-out marker consumed by KimiSoul. Kimi's
  379                  # request serializer omits it when no per-call override is supplied.
  380                  gen_kwargs["max_completion_tokens"] = (
  381                      max_completion_tokens if max_completion_tokens > 0 else None
  382                  )
  383                  break
  384
  385              if gen_kwargs:
  386                  chat_provider = chat_provider.with_generation_kwargs(**gen_kwargs)
  387          case "openai_legacy":
  388              from kosong.contrib.chat_provider.openai_legacy import OpenAILegacy
  389
  390              reasoning_key = (
  391                  provider.reasoning_key
  392                  if provider.reasoning_key is not None
  393                  else "reasoning_content"
  394              )
  395              chat_provider = OpenAILegacy(
  396                  model=model.model,
  397                  base_url=provider.base_url,
  398                  api_key=resolved_api_key,
  399                  reasoning_key=reasoning_key,
  400                  default_headers=dict(provider.custom_headers) if provider.custom_headers else None,
  401              )
  402          case "openai_responses":
  403              from kosong.contrib.chat_provider.openai_responses import OpenAIResponses
  404
  405              chat_provider = OpenAIResponses(
  406                  model=model.model,
  407                  base_url=provider.base_url,
  408                  api_key=resolved_api_key,
  409                  default_headers=dict(provider.custom_headers) if provider.custom_headers else None,
  410              )
  411          case "anthropic":
  412              from kosong.contrib.chat_provider.anthropic import Anthropic
  413
  414              chat_provider = Anthropic(
  415                  model=model.model,
  416                  base_url=provider.base_url,
  417                  api_key=resolved_api_key,
  418                  default_max_tokens=50000,
  419                  metadata={"user_id": session_id} if session_id else None,
  420                  default_headers=dict(provider.custom_headers) if provider.custom_headers else None,
  421              )
  422          case "google_genai" | "gemini":
  423              from kosong.contrib.chat_provider.google_genai import GoogleGenAI
  424
  425              chat_provider = GoogleGenAI(
  426                  model=model.model,
  427                  base_url=provider.base_url,
  428                  api_key=resolved_api_key,
  429                  default_headers=dict(provider.custom_headers) if provider.custom_headers else None,
  430              )
  431          case "vertexai":
  432              from kosong.contrib.chat_provider.google_genai import GoogleGenAI
  433
  434              os.environ.update(provider.env or {})
  435              chat_provider = GoogleGenAI(
  436                  model=model.model,
  437                  base_url=provider.base_url,
  438                  api_key=resolved_api_key,
  439                  vertexai=True,
  440                  default_headers=dict(provider.custom_headers) if provider.custom_headers else None,
  441              )
  442          case "_echo":
  443              from kosong.chat_provider.echo import EchoChatProvider
  444
  445              chat_provider = EchoChatProvider()
  446          case "_scripted_echo":
  447              from kosong.chat_provider.echo import ScriptedEchoChatProvider
  448
  449              if provider.env:
  450                  os.environ.update(provider.env)
  451              scripts = _load_scripted_echo_scripts()
  452              trace_value = os.getenv("KIMI_SCRIPTED_ECHO_TRACE", "")
  453              trace = trace_value.strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}
  454              chat_provider = ScriptedEchoChatProvider(scripts, trace=trace)
  455          case "_chaos":
  456              from kosong.chat_provider.chaos import ChaosChatProvider, ChaosConfig
  457              from kosong.chat_provider.kimi import Kimi
  458
  459              chat_provider = ChaosChatProvider(
  460                  provider=Kimi(
  461                      model=model.model,
  462                      base_url=provider.base_url,
  463                      api_key=resolved_api_key,
  464                      default_headers=_kimi_default_headers(provider, oauth),
  465                  ),
  466                  chaos_config=ChaosConfig(
  467                      error_probability=0.8,
  468                      error_types=[429, 500, 503],
  469                  ),
  470              )
  471
  472      capabilities = derive_model_capabilities(model)
  473
  474      # Apply thinking if specified or if model always requires thinking
  475      thinking_on = "always_thinking" in capabilities or (
  476          thinking is True and "thinking" in capabilities
  477      )
  478      if thinking_on:
  479          chat_provider = chat_provider.with_thinking("high")
  480      elif thinking is False:
  481          chat_provider = chat_provider.with_thinking("off")
  482      # If thinking is None and model doesn't always think, leave as-is (default behavior)
  483
  484      # Apply Moonshot-specific ``thinking.keep`` (preserved thinking) only when
  485      # the model is actually in thinking mode; otherwise the API would see a
  486      # ``thinking.keep`` without an accompanying ``thinking.type`` it honors.
  487      if thinking_on and provider.type == "kimi":
  488          from kosong.chat_provider.kimi import Kimi
  489
  490          if isinstance(chat_provider, Kimi) and (
  491              thinking_keep := os.getenv("KIMI_MODEL_THINKING_KEEP")
  492          ):
  493              chat_provider = chat_provider.with_extra_body({"thinking": {"keep": thinking_keep}})
  494
  495      return LLM(
  496          chat_provider=chat_provider,
  497          max_context_size=model.max_context_size,
  498          capabilities=capabilities,
  499          model_config=model,
  500          provider_config=provider,
  501      )

  504  def clone_llm_with_model_alias(
  505      llm: LLM | None,
  506      config: Config,
  507      model_alias: str | None,
  508      *,
  509      session_id: str,
  510      oauth: OAuthManager | None,
  511  ) -> LLM | None:
  512      if model_alias is None:
  513          return llm
  514      if model_alias not in config.models:
  515          raise KeyError(f"Unknown model alias: {model_alias}")
  516      model = config.models[model_alias]
  517      provider = config.providers[model.provider]
  518      thinking: bool | None = None
  519      if llm is not None:
  520          effort = getattr(llm.chat_provider, "thinking_effort", None)
  521          if effort is not None:
  522              thinking = effort != "off"
  523      return create_llm(
  524          provider,
  525          model,
  526          thinking=thinking,
  527          session_id=session_id,
  528          oauth=oauth,
  529      )

  531
  532  def derive_model_capabilities(model: LLMModel) -> set[ModelCapability]:
  533      capabilities = set(model.capabilities or ())
  534      # Models with "thinking" in their name are always-thinking models
  535      if "thinking" in model.model.lower() or "reason" in model.model.lower():
  536          capabilities.update(("thinking", "always_thinking"))
  537      # These models support thinking but can be toggled on/off
  538      elif model.model in {"kimi-for-coding", "kimi-code"}:
  539          capabilities.update(("thinking", "image_in", "video_in"))
  540      return capabilities

03
L1factmodelContext

03 · completion 上限按真实请求估算并留安全边际

源码事实

请求估算包含 system prompt、工具 schema、role/metadata、tool calls、media 与历史；Kimi completion cap 取配置预算和剩余 context 的较小值，主 step 还把 pending token floor 与 safety margin 算入。

白话解释

不只数聊天正文，还把工具说明书和图片等隐藏成本算进去，再决定模型最多还能写多少。

对自研 Harness 的含义

减少输出顶破窗口；估算仍非 provider tokenizer 的精确值。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 181–221
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 222–263
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 4a550effdfcb · 打开账本提交 ↗
src/kimi_cli/llm.py:181–263固定提交 ↗
  181  def compute_max_completion_tokens(
  182      *,
  183      max_context_size: int,
  184      input_tokens: int,
  185      response_budget: int | None,
  186      fallback_budget: int = DEFAULT_UNKNOWN_CONTEXT_COMPLETION_TOKENS,
  187  ) -> int:
  188      """Compute the Kimi completion cap from the hard cap and remaining context."""
  189      if max_context_size <= 0:
  190          return max(1, response_budget if response_budget is not None else fallback_budget)
  191
  192      input_tokens = max(0, input_tokens)
  193      remaining = max(1, max_context_size - input_tokens)
  194      requested = response_budget if response_budget is not None else max_context_size
  195      return max(1, min(requested, remaining))
  196
  197
  198  def estimate_request_tokens(
  199      system_prompt: str,
  200      tools: Sequence[Tool],
  201      history: Sequence[Message],
  202  ) -> int:
  203      """Estimate all token-bearing parts of a chat request.
  204
  205      The estimate is deliberately request-scoped: unlike ``Context.token_count_with_pending``,
  206      it includes the system prompt, tool schemas, message metadata, tool calls, and media. Exact
  207      tokenization is provider/model specific, so callers should still reserve a small safety
  208      margin when using the result to derive a hard completion limit.
  209      """
  210      return (
  211          _estimate_text_tokens(system_prompt)
  212          + sum(_estimate_tool_tokens(tool) for tool in tools)
  213          + sum(_estimate_message_tokens(message) for message in history)
  214      )
  215
  216
  217  def estimate_message_tokens(messages: Sequence[Message]) -> int:
  218      """Estimate token-bearing content for messages added outside the main context."""
  219      return sum(_estimate_message_tokens(message) for message in messages)
  220
  221
  222  def _estimate_text_tokens(text: str) -> int:
  223      ascii_count = sum(char.isascii() for char in text)
  224      non_ascii_count = len(text) - ascii_count
  225      return (ascii_count + 3) // 4 + non_ascii_count
  226
  227
  228  def _estimate_tool_tokens(tool: Tool) -> int:
  229      return (
  230          _estimate_text_tokens(tool.name)
  231          + _estimate_text_tokens(tool.description)
  232          + _estimate_text_tokens(
  233              json.dumps(tool.parameters, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"))
  234          )
  235      )
  236
  237
  238  def _estimate_message_tokens(message: Message) -> int:
  239      total = _estimate_text_tokens(message.role)
  240      if message.name:
  241          total += _estimate_text_tokens(message.name)
  242      if message.tool_call_id:
  243          total += _estimate_text_tokens(message.tool_call_id)
  244
  245      for part in message.content:
  246          if isinstance(part, TextPart):
  247              total += _estimate_text_tokens(part.text)
  248          elif isinstance(part, ThinkPart):
  249              total += _estimate_text_tokens(part.think)
  250          elif isinstance(part, (ImageURLPart, AudioURLPart, VideoURLPart)):
  251              total += MEDIA_TOKEN_ESTIMATE
  252          else:
  253              total += _estimate_text_tokens(part.model_dump_json(exclude_none=True))
  254
  255      for tool_call in message.tool_calls or ():
  256          total += _estimate_text_tokens(tool_call.id)
  257          total += _estimate_text_tokens(tool_call.function.name)
  258          total += _estimate_text_tokens(tool_call.function.arguments or "")
  259          if tool_call.extras:
  260              total += _estimate_text_tokens(
  261                  json.dumps(tool_call.extras, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"))
  262              )
  263      return total
  264
  265
  266  def model_display_name(model_name: str | None, model: LLMModel | None = None) -> str:
  267      if model is not None and model.display_name:
  268          return model.display_name
  269      if not model_name:
  270          return ""
  271      if model_name in ("kimi-for-coding", "kimi-code"):
  272          return "kimi-for-coding"
  273      return model_name
  274
  275
  276  def augment_provider_with_env_vars(provider: LLMProvider, model: LLMModel) -> dict[str, str]:
  277      """Override provider/model settings from environment variables.
  278
  279      Returns:
  280          Mapping of environment variables that were applied.
  281      """
  282      applied: dict[str, str] = {}
  283
  284      match provider.type:
  285          case "kimi":
  286              if base_url := os.getenv("KIMI_BASE_URL"):
  287                  provider.base_url = base_url
  288                  applied["KIMI_BASE_URL"] = base_url
  289              if api_key := os.getenv("KIMI_API_KEY"):
  290                  provider.api_key = SecretStr(api_key)
  291                  applied["KIMI_API_KEY"] = "******"
  292              if model_name := os.getenv("KIMI_MODEL_NAME"):
  293                  model.model = model_name
  294                  applied["KIMI_MODEL_NAME"] = model_name
  295              if max_context_size := os.getenv("KIMI_MODEL_MAX_CONTEXT_SIZE"):
  296                  model.max_context_size = int(max_context_size)
  297                  applied["KIMI_MODEL_MAX_CONTEXT_SIZE"] = max_context_size
  298              if capabilities := os.getenv("KIMI_MODEL_CAPABILITIES"):
  299                  caps_lower = (cap.strip().lower() for cap in capabilities.split(",") if cap.strip())
  300                  model.capabilities = set(
  301                      cast(ModelCapability, cap)
  302                      for cap in caps_lower
  303                      if cap in get_args(ModelCapability.__value__)
  304                  )
  305                  applied["KIMI_MODEL_CAPABILITIES"] = capabilities
  306          case "openai_legacy" | "openai_responses":
  307              if base_url := os.getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL"):
  308                  provider.base_url = base_url
  309              if api_key := os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"):
  310                  provider.api_key = SecretStr(api_key)
  311          case _:
  312              pass
  313
  314      return applied
  315

  317  def _kimi_default_headers(provider: LLMProvider, oauth: OAuthManager | None) -> dict[str, str]:
  318      headers = {"User-Agent": USER_AGENT}
  319      if oauth:
  320          headers.update(oauth.common_headers())
  321      if provider.custom_headers:
  322          headers.update(provider.custom_headers)
  323      return headers

04
L1factmodelContext

04 · 上下文是可增量恢复的 JSONL 事件账本

源码事实

Context 将普通消息与 _system_prompt、_checkpoint、_usage 记录写入同一 JSONL；restore 跳过 malformed/non-object/invalid record，重建 history、checkpoint cursor、最新 token count 和 usage 之后的 pending estimate。

白话解释

每条对话、存档点和 token 仪表读数都单独写一行；尾部坏一行不会让整场会话报废。

对自研 Harness 的含义

易审计、易增量追加；没有数据库事务，靠逐行容错和旋转文件恢复。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 20–41
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 42–65
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   20  class Context:
   21      def __init__(self, file_backend: Path):
   22          self._file_backend = file_backend
   23          self._history: list[Message] = []
   24          self._token_count: int = 0
   25          self._pending_token_estimate: int = 0
   26          self._next_checkpoint_id: int = 0
   27          """The ID of the next checkpoint, starting from 0, incremented after each checkpoint."""
   28          self._system_prompt: str | None = None
   29
   30      async def restore(self) -> bool:
   31          logger.debug("Restoring context from file: {file_backend}", file_backend=self._file_backend)
   32          if self._history:
   33              logger.error("The context storage is already modified")
   34              raise RuntimeError("The context storage is already modified")
   35          if not self._file_backend.exists():
   36              logger.debug("No context file found, skipping restoration")
   37              return False
   38          if self._file_backend.stat().st_size == 0:
   39              logger.debug("Empty context file, skipping restoration")
   40              return False
   41
   42          messages_after_last_usage: list[Message] = []
   43          async with aiofiles.open(self._file_backend, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
   44              line_no = 0
   45              async for line in f:
   46                  line_no += 1
   47                  if not line.strip():
   48                      continue
   49                  line_json = self._parse_context_line(
   50                      line,
   51                      file_backend=self._file_backend,
   52                      line_no=line_no,
   53                  )
   54                  if line_json is None:
   55                      continue
   56                  self._apply_context_record(
   57                      line_json,
   58                      history=self._history,
   59                      messages_after_last_usage=messages_after_last_usage,
   60                      file_backend=self._file_backend,
   61                      line_no=line_no,
   62                  )
   63
   64          self._pending_token_estimate = estimate_text_tokens(messages_after_last_usage)
   65          return True
   66
   67      @property
   68      def history(self) -> Sequence[Message]:
   69          return self._history
   70
   71      @property
   72      def token_count(self) -> int:
   73          return self._token_count
   74
   75      @property
   76      def token_count_with_pending(self) -> int:
   77          return self._token_count + self._pending_token_estimate
   78
   79      @property
   80      def n_checkpoints(self) -> int:
   81          return self._next_checkpoint_id

   85          return self._system_prompt

   88      def file_backend(self) -> Path:
   89          return self._file_backend

   91      async def write_system_prompt(self, prompt: str) -> None:
   92          """Write the system prompt as the first record of the context file.
   93
   94          If the file is empty, writes it directly. If the file already has content
   95          (e.g. a legacy session without system prompt), prepends it atomically via a
   96          temporary file to avoid corruption on crash and avoid loading the entire file
   97          into memory.
   98          """
   99          prompt_line = json.dumps({"role": "_system_prompt", "content": prompt}) + "\n"
  100
  101          def _write_system_prompt_sync() -> None:
  102              if not self._file_backend.exists() or self._file_backend.stat().st_size == 0:
  103                  self._file_backend.write_text(prompt_line, encoding="utf-8")
  104                  return
  105
  106              tmp_path = self._file_backend.with_suffix(".tmp")
  107              with (
  108                  tmp_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as tmp_f,
  109                  self._file_backend.open(encoding="utf-8") as src_f,
  110              ):
  111                  tmp_f.write(prompt_line)
  112                  while True:
  113                      chunk = src_f.read(64 * 1024)
  114                      if not chunk:
  115                          break
  116                      tmp_f.write(chunk)
  117              tmp_path.replace(self._file_backend)
  118
  119          await asyncio.to_thread(_write_system_prompt_sync)
  120
  121          self._system_prompt = prompt
  122
  123      async def checkpoint(self, add_user_message: bool):
  124          checkpoint_id = self._next_checkpoint_id
  125          self._next_checkpoint_id += 1
  126          logger.debug("Checkpointing, ID: {id}", id=checkpoint_id)
  127
  128          async with aiofiles.open(self._file_backend, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
  129              await f.write(json.dumps({"role": "_checkpoint", "id": checkpoint_id}) + "\n")
  130          if add_user_message:
  131              await self.append_message(
  132                  Message(role="user", content=[system(f"CHECKPOINT {checkpoint_id}")])
  133              )
  134
  135      async def revert_to(self, checkpoint_id: int):
  136          """

05
L1factmodelContext

05 · checkpoint 回退会保留旧文件并重算状态

源码事实

revert_to 先把当前 context 文件旋转为备份，再逐行复制到指定 checkpoint 之前，重新构建 history/usage/system prompt/checkpoint 与 pending estimate；clear 也先旋转而非直接覆盖。

白话解释

读档时旧账本不会消失，而是改名存档；新账本只抄到目标存档点。

对自研 Harness 的含义

误回退可人工取回旋转文件；磁盘占用会随压缩/回退累计。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 123–160
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 161–200
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 4a550effdfcb · 打开账本提交 ↗

  135      async def revert_to(self, checkpoint_id: int):
  136          """
  137          Revert the context to the specified checkpoint.
  138          After this, the specified checkpoint and all subsequent content will be
  139          removed from the context. File backend will be rotated.
  140
  141          Args:
  142              checkpoint_id (int): The ID of the checkpoint to revert to. 0 is the first checkpoint.
  143
  144          Raises:
  145              ValueError: When the checkpoint does not exist.
  146              RuntimeError: When no available rotation path is found.
  147          """
  148
  149          logger.debug("Reverting checkpoint, ID: {id}", id=checkpoint_id)
  150          if checkpoint_id >= self._next_checkpoint_id:
  151              logger.error("Checkpoint {checkpoint_id} does not exist", checkpoint_id=checkpoint_id)
  152              raise ValueError(f"Checkpoint {checkpoint_id} does not exist")
  153
  154          # rotate the context file
  155          rotated_file_path = await next_available_rotation(self._file_backend)
  156          if rotated_file_path is None:
  157              logger.error("No available rotation path found")
  158              raise RuntimeError("No available rotation path found")
  159          await aiofiles.os.replace(self._file_backend, rotated_file_path)
  160          logger.debug(
  161              "Rotated context file: {rotated_file_path}", rotated_file_path=rotated_file_path
  162          )
  163
  164          # restore the context until the specified checkpoint
  165          self._history.clear()
  166          self._token_count = 0
  167          self._next_checkpoint_id = 0
  168          self._system_prompt = None
  169          messages_after_last_usage: list[Message] = []
  170          async with (
  171              aiofiles.open(rotated_file_path, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as old_file,
  172              aiofiles.open(self._file_backend, "w", encoding="utf-8") as new_file,
  173          ):
  174              line_no = 0
  175              async for line in old_file:
  176                  line_no += 1
  177                  if not line.strip():
  178                      continue
  179
  180                  line_json = self._parse_context_line(
  181                      line,
  182                      file_backend=rotated_file_path,
  183                      line_no=line_no,
  184                  )
  185                  if line_json is None:
  186                      continue
  187                  if line_json.get("role") == "_checkpoint" and line_json.get("id") == checkpoint_id:
  188                      break
  189
  190                  keep_line = self._apply_context_record(
  191                      line_json,
  192                      history=self._history,
  193                      messages_after_last_usage=messages_after_last_usage,
  194                      file_backend=rotated_file_path,
  195                      line_no=line_no,
  196                  )
  197                  if keep_line:
  198                      await new_file.write(line)
  199
  200          self._pending_token_estimate = estimate_text_tokens(messages_after_last_usage)
  201
  202      async def clear(self):
  203          """
  204          Clear the context history.
  205          This is almost equivalent to revert_to(0), but without relying on the assumption
  206          that the first checkpoint exists.
  207          File backend will be rotated.
  208
  209          Raises:
  210              RuntimeError: When no available rotation path is found.
  211          """
  212
  213          logger.debug("Clearing context")
  214
  215          # rotate the context file
  216          rotated_file_path = await next_available_rotation(self._file_backend)
  217          if rotated_file_path is None:
  218              logger.error("No available rotation path found")
  219              raise RuntimeError("No available rotation path found")
  220          await aiofiles.os.replace(self._file_backend, rotated_file_path)
  221          self._file_backend.touch()
  222          logger.debug(
  223              "Rotated context file: {rotated_file_path}", rotated_file_path=rotated_file_path
  224          )
  225
  226          self._history.clear()
  227          self._token_count = 0
  228          self._pending_token_estimate = 0
  229          self._next_checkpoint_id = 0
  230          self._system_prompt = None
  231
  232      async def append_message(self, message: Message | Sequence[Message]):
  233          logger.debug("Appending message(s) to context: {message}", message=message)
  234          messages = [message] if isinstance(message, Message) else message
  235          self._history.extend(messages)
  236          self._pending_token_estimate += estimate_text_tokens(messages)
  237
  238          async with aiofiles.open(self._file_backend, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
  239              for message in messages:
  240                  await f.write(message.model_dump_json(exclude_none=True) + "\n")
  241
  242      async def update_token_count(self, token_count: int):
  243          logger.debug("Updating token count in context: {token_count}", token_count=token_count)
  244          self._token_count = token_count
  245          self._pending_token_estimate = 0
  246
  247          async with aiofiles.open(self._file_backend, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
  248              await f.write(json.dumps({"role": "_usage", "token_count": token_count}) + "\n")

  250      def _parse_context_line(
  251          self,
  252          line: str,
  253          *,

  261                  "Skipping malformed context line {line_no} in {file}: {error}",

  264                  error=exc,
  265              )
  266              return None
  267          if not isinstance(line_json, dict):
  268              logger.warning(
  269                  "Skipping non-object context line {line_no} in {file}",
  270                  line_no=line_no,
  271                  file=file_backend,

06
L1factmodelContext

06 · 压缩由阈值触发，摘要旧历史并保留最近轮次

源码事实

should_auto_compact 同时检查 context ratio 和 reserved context；SimpleCompaction 默认保留最近两组 user/assistant，把更早历史交 EmptyToolset 摘要、去除 thinking，再用 compaction prefix + summary + preserved messages 重建 context。

白话解释

箱子快满时，把旧账压成一页摘要，最近几轮原样保留；摘要过程不能再调用工具。

对自研 Harness 的含义

实现简单稳定，但摘要是有损的，字符/4 估算对 CJK 偏低，代码中也明确承认。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 37–58
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 59–82
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   37          The estimate is intentionally conservative — it will be replaced by the
   38          real value on the next LLM call.
   39          """
   40          if self.usage is not None and len(self.messages) > 0:
   41              summary_tokens = self.usage.output
   42              preserved_tokens = estimate_text_tokens(self.messages[1:])
   43              return summary_tokens + preserved_tokens
   44
   45          return estimate_text_tokens(self.messages)
   46
   47
   48  def estimate_text_tokens(messages: Sequence[Message]) -> int:
   49      """Estimate tokens from message text content using a character-based heuristic."""
   50      total_chars = 0
   51      for msg in messages:
   52          for part in msg.content:
   53              if isinstance(part, TextPart):
   54                  total_chars += len(part.text)
   55      # ~4 chars per token for English; somewhat underestimates for CJK text,
   56      # but this is a temporary estimate that gets corrected on the next LLM call.
   57      return total_chars // 4
   58
   59
   60  def should_auto_compact(
   61      token_count: int,
   62      max_context_size: int,
   63      *,
   64      trigger_ratio: float,
   65      reserved_context_size: int,
   66  ) -> bool:
   67      """Determine whether auto-compaction should be triggered.
   68
   69      Returns True when either condition is met (whichever fires first):
   70      - Ratio-based: token_count >= max_context_size * trigger_ratio
   71      - Reserved-based: token_count + reserved_context_size >= max_context_size
   72      """
   73      return (
   74          token_count >= max_context_size * trigger_ratio
   75          or token_count + reserved_context_size >= max_context_size
   76      )
   77
   78

  110  class SimpleCompaction:
  111      def __init__(self, max_preserved_messages: int = 2) -> None:
  112          self.max_preserved_messages = max_preserved_messages
  113
  114      async def compact(
  115          self,
  116          messages: Sequence[Message],
  117          llm: LLM,
  118          *,
  119          custom_instruction: str = "",
  120      ) -> CompactionResult:
  121          compact_message, to_preserve = self.prepare(messages, custom_instruction=custom_instruction)
  122          if compact_message is None:
  123              return CompactionResult(messages=to_preserve, usage=None)
  124
  125          logger.debug("Compacting context...")
  126          result = await kosong.step(
  127              chat_provider=llm.chat_provider,
  128              system_prompt=COMPACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
  129              toolset=EmptyToolset(),
  130              history=[compact_message],
  131          )
  132          if result.usage:
  133              logger.debug(
  134                  "Compaction used {input} input tokens and {output} output tokens",
  135                  input=result.usage.input,
  136                  output=result.usage.output,
  137              )
  138
  139          content: list[ContentPart] = [system(COMPACTION_OUTPUT_PREFIX)]
  140          compacted_msg = result.message
  141
  142          # drop thinking parts if any
  143          content.extend(part for part in compacted_msg.content if not isinstance(part, ThinkPart))
  144          compacted_messages: list[Message] = [Message(role="user", content=content)]
  145          compacted_messages.extend(to_preserve)
  146          return CompactionResult(
  147              messages=compacted_messages, usage=result.usage, trace_id=result.trace_id
  148          )

  151          compact_message: Message | None

07
L1factmodelContext

07 · 压缩后显式恢复活动后台任务与动态约束状态

源码事实

root compaction 前估算摘要后请求预算；压缩成功后 clear/重写 system prompt/checkpoint/messages，再追加 active task snapshot、重设 token count、通知 injection providers reset，并发 CompactionEnd/telemetry/PostCompact。

白话解释

摘要不会让正在跑的 build/test 凭空消失；压缩后会重新贴一张活动任务清单，并让 Plan/AFK 提醒重新校准。

对自研 Harness 的含义

避免长后台任务在摘要后失联；活动任务快照本身也占上下文。

01
L1facttools

01 · 工具表由 agent spec 动态装配，插件和 MCP 追加进入同一 Toolset

源码事实

load_agent 从 YAML 选 tools/allowed_tools 并应用 exclude_tools，通过依赖注入实例化；插件冲突时跳过；MCP config 验证后可后台加载或延迟到首轮。

白话解释

工具箱不是写死在循环里，而是由角色配置装配；本地插件和远程 MCP 最终都变成模型看到的同类工具。

对自研 Harness 的含义

角色最小权限容易表达；工具名称冲突按先到内建优先。

  494  def _load_system_prompt(
  495      path: Path, args: dict[str, str], builtin_args: BuiltinSystemPromptArgs
  496  ) -> str:
  497      logger.info("Loading system prompt: {path}", path=path)
  498      system_prompt = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
  499      logger.debug(
  500          "Substituting system prompt with builtin args: {builtin_args}, spec args: {spec_args}",
  501          builtin_args=builtin_args,
  502          spec_args=args,
  503      )
  504      env = JinjaEnvironment(
  505          loader=FileSystemLoader(path.parent),
  506          keep_trailing_newline=True,
  507          lstrip_blocks=True,
  508          trim_blocks=True,
  509          variable_start_string="${",
  510          variable_end_string="}",
  511          undefined=StrictUndefined,
  512      )
  513      try:
  514          template = env.from_string(system_prompt)
  515          return template.render(asdict(builtin_args), **args)
  516      except UndefinedError as exc:
  517          raise SystemPromptTemplateError(f"Missing system prompt arg in {path}: {exc}") from exc
  518      except TemplateError as exc:
  519          raise SystemPromptTemplateError(f"Invalid system prompt template: {path}: {exc}") from exc
  520

02
L1facttools

02 · 同一模型回复里的不同工具并发启动，完全重复调用共享结果

源码事实

Kosong 收到完整 tool call 即调用 Toolset.handle；KimiToolset 为每个新 call 创建 asyncio task。同 step 相同 name+canonical args 不重复执行，而是等待原 task 并复制 result。

白话解释

模型一口气开多张不同工单会一起跑；两张完全相同的工单只做一次，第二张拿同一结果。

对自研 Harness 的含义

吞吐高且减少重复副作用；不同工具之间没有全局串行保证。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 134–149
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 150–167
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

packages/kosong/src/kosong/__init__.py:134–167固定提交 ↗
  134      tool_calls: list[ToolCall] = []
  135      tool_result_futures: dict[str, ToolResultFuture] = {}
  136
  137      def future_done_callback(future: ToolResultFuture):
  138          if on_tool_result:
  139              try:
  140                  result = future.result()
  141                  on_tool_result(result)
  142              except asyncio.CancelledError:
  143                  return
  144
  145      async def on_tool_call(tool_call: ToolCall):
  146          tool_calls.append(tool_call)
  147          result = toolset.handle(tool_call)
  148
  149          if isinstance(result, ToolResult):
  150              future = ToolResultFuture()
  151              future.add_done_callback(future_done_callback)
  152              future.set_result(result)
  153              tool_result_futures[tool_call.id] = future
  154          else:
  155              result.add_done_callback(future_done_callback)
  156              tool_result_futures[tool_call.id] = result
  157
  158      try:
  159          result = await generate(
  160              chat_provider,
  161              system_prompt,
  162              toolset.tools,
  163              history,
  164              on_message_part=on_message_part,
  165              on_tool_call=on_tool_call,
  166              on_trace_id=on_trace_id,
  167          )
  168      except (ChatProviderError, asyncio.CancelledError):
  169          # cancel all the futures to avoid hanging tasks
  170          for future in tool_result_futures.values():
  171              future.remove_done_callback(future_done_callback)
  172              future.cancel()
  173          await asyncio.gather(*tool_result_futures.values(), return_exceptions=True)
  174          raise
  175
  176      return StepResult(
  177          result.id,
  178          result.message,
  179          result.usage,
  180          tool_calls,
  181          tool_result_futures,
  182          trace_id=result.trace_id,
  183      )

  186  @dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
  187  class StepResult:
  188      id: str | None
  189      """The ID of the generated message."""
  190
  191      message: Message
  192      """The message generated in this step."""
  193
  194      usage: TokenUsage | None
  195      """The token usage in this step."""

  198      """All the tool calls generated in this step."""

  204      """The ``x-trace-id`` response header of the request, if the provider exposes it."""
  205
  206      async def tool_results(self) -> list[ToolResult]:
  207          """All the tool results returned by corresponding tool calls."""
  208          if not self._tool_result_futures:
  209              return []
  210
  211          try:
  212              results: list[ToolResult] = []
  213              for tool_call in self.tool_calls:
  214                  future = self._tool_result_futures[tool_call.id]
  215                  result = await future
  216                  results.append(result)
  217              return results
  218          finally:
  219              # one exception should cancel all the futures to avoid hanging tasks
  220              for future in self._tool_result_futures.values():
  221                  future.cancel()
  222              await asyncio.gather(*self._tool_result_futures.values(), return_exceptions=True)
  223

03
L1facttools

03 · 跨 step 重复调用按 3/5/8/12 阶梯提醒并强停

源码事实

Toolset 对 canonical tool call 维护连续 streak：第 3 次开始提示换方法，第 5 次展示调用细节，第 8 次要求停止工具并总结，第 12 次设置 force_stop_turn。

白话解释

不是第一次重复就封杀，而是逐级提醒；连续十二次原地打转才硬踩刹车。

对自研 Harness 的含义

降低模型卡死和费用失控，也允许合理重试；canonicalization 只比较名称和 JSON 参数，不判断外部状态是否变化。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 116–143
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 144–172
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  153  _REPEAT_REMINDER_1_START = 3
  154  _REPEAT_REMINDER_2_START = 5
  155  _REPEAT_REMINDER_3_START = 8
  156  _REPEAT_FORCE_STOP_STREAK = 12
  157
  158  type RepeatAction = Literal["none", "r1", "r2", "r3", "stop"]
  159
  160
  161  def _build_repeat_reminder(
  162      streak: int, tool_name: str, canonical_args: str
  163  ) -> tuple[RepeatAction, str | None]:
  164      if streak >= _REPEAT_FORCE_STOP_STREAK:
  165          return "stop", _REMINDER_TEXT_3
  166      if streak >= _REPEAT_REMINDER_3_START:
  167          return "r3", _REMINDER_TEXT_3
  168      if streak >= _REPEAT_REMINDER_2_START:
  169          return "r2", _make_reminder_text_2(tool_name, streak, canonical_args)
  170      if streak >= _REPEAT_REMINDER_1_START:
  171          return "r1", _REMINDER_TEXT_1
  172      return "none", None
  173
  174
  175  def _sort_json_value(value: object) -> object:
  176      if isinstance(value, list):
  177          return [_sort_json_value(item) for item in cast("list[object]", value)]
  178      if isinstance(value, dict):
  179          value_dict = cast("dict[str, object]", value)
  180          return {key: _sort_json_value(value_dict[key]) for key in sorted(value_dict)}
  181      return value
  182
  183
  184  def _canonical_tool_arguments(arguments: Any) -> str:
  185      try:
  186          return json.dumps(
  187              _sort_json_value(arguments),
  188              ensure_ascii=False,
  189              separators=(",", ":"),
  190          )
  191      except (TypeError, ValueError):
  192          return str(arguments)
  193
  194
  195  def _canonical_tool_arguments_text(arguments: str) -> str:
  196      try:
  197          return _canonical_tool_arguments(json.loads(arguments, strict=False))
  198      except json.JSONDecodeError:
  199          return arguments
  200
  201
  202  def _normalize_call_key(tool_name: str, arguments: str) -> ToolCallKey:
  203      return (tool_name, _canonical_tool_arguments_text(arguments))
  204
  205
  206  def _append_reminder_to_return_value(
  207      return_value: Any, reminder_text: str = _REMINDER_TEXT_1
  208  ) -> Any:
  209      """Append dedup reminder text to a ToolReturnValue output."""
  210      from kosong.tooling import ToolReturnValue
  211
  212      if not isinstance(return_value, ToolReturnValue):
  213          return return_value
  214
  215      output = return_value.output
  216
  217      if isinstance(output, str):
  218          new_output = output + reminder_text
  219      else:
  220          new_output = list(output)
  221          if new_output and isinstance(new_output[-1], TextPart):
  222              new_output[-1] = TextPart(text=new_output[-1].text + reminder_text)
  223          else:
  224              new_output.append(TextPart(text=reminder_text))
  225
  226      return return_value.model_copy(update={"output": new_output})
  227
